Prediction: b b* a*
Reasoning: All positive strings consist only of b’s possibly followed by a’s, with at least one leading b. No positive contains c, and any a must appear only after all b’s. Hence the language is “one or more b’s then any number of a’s”.

Epoch 1, initial retry
Prediction: (a + b)* + (c + b)*
Reasoning: All positives never contain both a and c in the same string; they consist only of a’s and b’s, or only of c’s and b’s (or just b’s or empty). Negatives always have both a and c, so the language is the union of strings over {a,b} and strings over {c,b}.

Epoch 2, initial retry

This excludes any occurrence of “bc”, “cc”, “ac” or the substring “aba”.

Epoch 3, initial retry

Reasoning: The positives are exactly the strings over {a,b} that never contain the substring “aba”, optionally prefixed by a single c (which itself may not be followed by any c). Thus the language is X ∪ cX, where X = b* or b* followed by a block of a’s possibly separated by runs of at least two b’s.

Accuracy at epoch 4: 0
Epoch 5, initial retry

Reasoning: The language consists of any number of b’s, optionally followed by a sequence of a and c where a c never appears twice in a row, and after this sequence only b’s may follow. This forbids the substrings “b c”, “c c”, “a b⁺ a” and “c b⁺ a”.
